Every GTA 6 ending and how to get them

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

All the endings (and a couple variations) for GTA 6 we've seen so far.

When you purchase through links on our site, we may earn an affiliate commission. Here’s how it works.

Obviously, massive spoilers follow for GTA 6 endings!

There are multiple endings to GTA 6, and while we haven't experienced them all personally here at PC Gamer, the gaming community has been sharing them around for anyone curious about what they may have missed.

The ending you get depends on the choices you make during the final GTA 6 story mission, but also depends on how you handled Jason and Lucia's romance and criminal profiles throughout the game, so seeing every version of the ending would mean not just playing the ending multiple times, but having different playthroughs of the game altogether.

There are a couple of endings that I don't consider "different" from each other but do have some differences leading up to them, which I'll explain below. There may also be additional endings we haven't seen yet, so we may update this guide in the future.

In the bridge endings, Jason and Lucia have defeated Mendez and his squad and gotten the hard drive back from Georgio's penthouse and escaped. They drive to Cal's place in Key Lento, but discover he has been compromised. They drive to the bridge, but the cops have set up a roadblock and have the couple trapped. Lucia wants to keep going, but Jason suggests they jump off the bridge and swim for it.

A choice comes up on the screen: "Swim For it" or "No Surrender."

Jason and Lucia try to run the roadblock and are shot to death by cops on the bridge. They die holding hands. In the credits, we see that their son Tomas lives happily with Lucia's aunt, her husband, and their children.

Jason and Lucia are shot to death by cops on the bridge. They die holding hands. In the credits, their lawyer, Abe, brings the money to Lucia's aunt. Tomas lives happily with Lucia's aunt, her husband, and their children in a much bigger house than the other No Surrender ending.

Choosing "Swim for it" presents an additional choice. When Jason gets out of the car, Lucia can choose to "Exit" the car with him, or change her mind by not choosing it.

Jason and Lucia decide to jump off the bridge and swim for it. They both exit the car without holding guns. Lucia jumps, but Jason doesn't jump and is arrested. Lucia signs a deal for immunity while Jason goes to prison. Lucia gets a job in a diner. Lucia takes Tomas to meet Jason in prison, but he can only see his son through a glass divider.

Jason and Lucia decide to jump off the bridge and swim for it. Jason exits the car with a gun, but Lucia changes her mind and stays in the car. The police open fire and Jason and Lucia are both shot to death. Tomas lives with Lucia's aunt, her husband, and their children.

Jason and Lucia decide to jump off the bridge and swim for it, Jason doesn't jump and is arrested. Two years later, Lucia uses the savings account to get Jason freed from prison. Lucia goes to the prison to pick up Jason. Jason meets Tomas and holds his hand.

If you don't have a strong relationship between Jason and Lucia, there is a different ending location: the base of Georgio's tower. In these endings, the couple never makes it to the bridge. You can also choose to break up the couple, either as Jason or as Lucia, as you begin the mission Protective Custody.

After the tower fight with Georgio's men, police confront the couple at the base of the tower. The police open fire and Jason pushes Lucia down. Jason is shot. Lucia has a choice to "Stay down" or "Defend Jason". She stays down and watches Jason die. Lucia is arrested, signs a deal, and visits Tomas at her aunt's house. Lucia then leaves Tomas with her aunt, takes out a gun, and continues her life of crime by preparing to rob a diner.

After the tower fight with Georgio's men, police confront them at the base of the tower. The police open fire and Jason pushes Lucia down. Jason is shot. Lucia chooses "Defend Jason" and fights the cops. They die together.

On the way down to ground level at the tower, Jason decides to run. Lucia is arrested and signs the deal, then lives with her aunt and Tomas while working in a diner. One night there's a knock on the door and she finds a bag of cash on the porch. We see Jason driving away with... Rocco the drug-sniffing dog from earlier in the game. Sure, that might as well happen.

How to get this ending (sourced by GamingwithTZ on YouTube):

Jason and Lucia make a deal with Mendez after defeating him. Once they have the hard drive, they're surrounded by cops at the bottom of the tower. Mendez arrives and takes them away in his car, then drives them to the swamp. Later in the credits, we see two skulls in the swamp, side-by-side, with a bullet hole in each.

Note: You may need a specific criminal profile for this ending. I tried to take the deal (as Lucia) in one of my ending playthroughs, but Jason shot Mendez anyway. I had both Jason and Lucia at "Professional" level criminal profiles. This ending was not available to me. Speculation, but this ending may only be available if you don't have "Professional" as your criminal profiles.

There are potentially (and almost certainly) more endings to GTA 6. There are still a few combinations of all the different variables, including your romance level, criminal profile level, amount of money saved, and the choices you make during the final mission, that we haven't seen yet.

We'll update this guide if the community finds more endings.

Arsenal pose major Champions League test for Dortmund, says Kovac

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Syndicated reporting provided by Stats Perform and reviewed by the SoccerNews editorial team.

Niko Kovac was under no illusions about the size of the task awaiting his Borussia Dortmund players ahead of facing Arsenal in the Champions League.

Dortmund make the trip to the Emirates Stadium fresh from beating Borussia Monchengladbach on Saturday, a result that moved them two points clear at the Bundesliga summit.

BVB have started a Bundesliga season unbeaten in their first 10 matches for only the third time, after 2002-03 and 2018-19, while their 24 points at this stage is just one fewer than their club record (25 points in 2010-11 and 2013-14).

However, they face an Arsenal side six points clear at the top of the Premier League despite Mikel Arteta's side drawing 2-2 with Newcastle United at the weekend.

The Gunners are also unbeaten in their last 19 Champions League games (W14 D5 – excluding penalty shootout defeats).

Should they avoid defeat here, they will be just the third English team to go 20+ games without losing in the European Cup/Champions League, after Manchester United (25 – September 2007–May 2009) and Manchester City (26 – September 2022–October 2024).

Dortmund also have a miserable record in England, losing eight of their last nine Champions League away games when facing off against sides from the Premier League.

Arsenal have won three of their four Champions League home games against Borussia Dortmund (L1). Their only loss was in October 2013, with Robert Lewandowski scoring a late winner for the visitors in a 2-1 victory.

"We are not only playing against the best defence in England and Europe, but also against the reigning English champions, Champions League finalists last season and the current Premier League leaders," Kovac told reporters.

"We're aware how difficult this is going to be. They have a great team and a great coach. But we're also difficult to play against, and we'll have our chances."

Home of The Arsenal. https://t.co/qefVsPBSej

— Borussia Dortmund (@BlackYellow) November 23, 2026

Kovac has been handed a double injury boost ahead of facing Arsenal, with the Dortmund boss revealing both Serhou Guirassy and Carney Chukwuemeka are available.

Guirassy, who is BVB's top scorer in all competitions with eight goals in 14 appearances, sustained a hamstring strain just before the international break, while Chukwuemeka has recovered from a thigh issue.

"They're ready for tomorrow's match," added the BVB boss. "They're available for selection. We'll have to wait and see whether they'll start or come on as the match progresses."

Kovac was also asked about the array of English talent that have come through Dortmund's ranks, with the likes of Jude Bellingham and Jadon Sancho enjoying successful spells during their time in Germany.

Frank Lampard and Wayne Bridge for Chelsea in April 2004 (at Highbury) and Harry Kane for Bayern Munich in April 2024 (Emirates Stadium) are the only English players to have scored a Champions League away goal at Arsenal.

Jobe Bellingham and Ethan Nwaneri have both appeared in all four of Dortmund's games in the competition this season, although both are still yet to score.

Nwaneri is cleared to play due to UEFA's rules that allow loan players to play against their parent clubs, with the Arsenal forward yet to register a goal involvement across his 13 appearances in all competitions.

"The Bundesliga is a top league where young talent can come through, especially at Dortmund," Kovac said. "As a club, we are known for developing younger players.

"The English players are very strong physically, technically at the highest level, but the Premier League is the best league in the world and they need playing time.

"A move to Germany, especially Dortmund, is a very good step."

De Bruyne not focused on Napoli exit rumours ahead of Man City reunion

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Syndicated reporting provided by Stats Perform and reviewed by the SoccerNews editorial team.

Kevin De Bruyne insisted the "unpleasant" rumours surrounding his future at Napoli would be put to one side ahead of his return to Manchester City on Tuesday.

De Bruyne returns to the Etihad Stadium for the second straight season for another Champions League encounter, but the Belgian has been the subject of speculation.

It has been reported that the 35-year-old, who only joined the Serie A giants from Man City last summer, could be on his way out as early as the January transfer window.

PSV has been touted as a possible destination for De Bruyne, with the midfielder out of contract at the end of the 2026-27 campaign.

De Bruyne's wife revealed in a recent magazine interview that she would one day like to return to Belgium, though De Bruyne said her words were misinterpreted by the Italian media and was keen to put the record straight.

"I'm quite sorry about it all, to be honest. If you go and read the article which you have not read, you will understand what she said," De Bruyne told reporters.

"I was away in Belgium and then a lot of things were being said and there were a lot of negative reactions towards my wife.

"I saw photos of my house in the press, which is not good. I know that journalists in Italy have created this situation and I don't know why.

"You do not know my private life, you have no right to comment on my private life. It was an unpleasant situation for me and my wife."

When pressed further on his future in Naples, De Bruyne added: "I can't say very much at this point. I've played at 400% so far.

"I'm not 20 years old anymore, I'm almost 36, so there are lots of things to consider.

"Right now, there's not a lot to say. I'm playing well, I'm trying to help the team to do well. There have been lots of challenges this year and a lot of things have happened."

Squad for Manchester City v Napoli https://t.co/E9kZvZPQTj

— Official SSC Napoli (@en_sscnapoli) November 23, 2026

De Bruyne will be hoping to play a bigger part in Napoli's next trip to his former employers, having been substituted early on following Giovanni Di Lorenzo's red card.

City won 2-0 that day thanks to goals from Erling Haaland and Jeremy Doku, with City unbeaten at home against Napoli (W2 D1).

De Bruyne was directly involved in 41 goals in 72 Champions League appearances for City (16 goals, 25 assists), the joint second-most for the club along with Haaland (41 in 42 games), with the pair only trailing Sergio Aguero (43).

He registered 108 goals and provided 170 assists in 422 appearances in all competitions during a trophy-laden spell with City.

City were found guilty of more than 100 breaches of the Premier League's financial regulations between 2009-10 and 2017-18 in September, and they have since appealed the ruling. With the appeal still ongoing, the potential punishments should the verdict be upheld have not been revealed.

"This is my club, I love the owners. I've nothing but affection for this club and that will never change," De Bruyne said of City's situation.

"Of course, in life there are rules, everybody plays on the limit, I would say, and for me, I play between the white lines and I compete with other players and I have had a lot of experiences with my teams, with the players, so for me that is the thing that I value most.

"And even later on in my career, I think the most important is the relationships between everybody that I had and how you manage these things.

"I've given my all and that is not going to change in my mind I don't think. Other people will change in their mind how they view me as a player or how we played but everybody has their own opinion I guess."

For Some Artists, Residency-Hopping Is a Form of Survival

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Artists are increasingly turning to the programs as sites of refuge as they navigate precarity, commercial pressures, and burnout.

For Paul Maheke, a French-born performance artist in his 40s, a year of funding, a sunlit studio in a sixteenth-century palatial estate in Rome, and ample time alone represented a new phase in a career that has long been shaped by transience. Over the last decade, before landing at the Villa Medici, he’d completed more than 14 artist residencies.

By 2015, after graduate school, he began moving between residencies every few months, relocating to remote locations in France, Vienna, and Canada. He sought distance from London’s commercial gallery circuit, a networking scene he described as populated with “opening-rats” and an environment he felt was depleting his focus. “Residencies were a way around that,” he told Hyperallergic, four months into his stay at the Italian complex. “I think I’ve never been sedentary. I tend to think better when I’m in movement. As I’m traveling, it’s always shifting my way of working.”

As his ideas developed, Maheke said that the pressure to hold down a studio space better suited to a painter no longer made sense. His work had outgrown a conventional studio, encompassing choreographed performances with professional dancers and drawing on autobiography. He described enmeshing an “Afro-descendant European perspective” with ideas about queerness, memory, and the body as a vessel for social experience.

Across countries and career stages, artists like Maheke have found themselves increasingly turning to residencies to navigate precarity. In interviews with Hyperallergic, artists reported using residencies as alternatives for permanent studios and refuges from the commercial pressures of major cultural centers. What were once considered philanthropic sites for creative retreat or intellectual leisure have become for many a workaround for periods of economic volatility and increased pressure to sustain artistic output.

The foundation invites applications from mission-aligned arts and science organizations for grants of up to $20,000 through March 31, 2027.

Selena Gomez’s Suede Bag Is the Ultimate Holiday Party Accessory—It’s Under $100 Right Now

This suede-wrapped style just made the top of my wish list.

10+ Winter Looks to Recreate From Fashion’s Beloved Style Icons

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Lifestyle  |  Read Source →

A little wardrobe inspiration is always welcome, especially when transitioning between seasons. As we move into winter, what better way to reinvigorate your wardrobe than by revisiting the style signifiers of some of fashion’s iconic muses? From Jacqueline Kennedy Onassis and Lee Radziwill to Carolyn Bessette-Kennedy, their sartorial signatures remain as relevant today as ever.

For holiday dressing inspiration, look no further than Bianca Jagger and Sade for bold ensembles to recreate. For timeless winter layering tips, pay close attention to Ali McGraw and Iman; they shared a love for versatile neutrals (and knew when to rely on a statement belt for added punch). Off-duty ideas come courtesy of Princess Diana and Jane Birkin, who both had an affinity for dressing up more casual pieces.

Winter is a season where artful layers and rich textures take center stage—notes these women knew how to hit. Their enduring looks, which we’re breaking down for you below, are proof.

Jackie O knew the power of a statement coat—here, she went for a knee-length, animal-print style with layered knits peeking out from underneath for contrast. It’s a timeless yet modern look that’s easy enough to piece together, just pair with a fitted pencil skirt and heeled ballerinas or loafers.

For folklore flair, style a velvet duster coat with a turtleneck, corduroy trousers, and statement belt. Flat leather boots and a slouchy beanie are the finishing touches.

Of course, Lee Radiziwill was an early proponent of the pankou closure jacket which you’re probably seeing everywhere this season. Recreate her look for an 8 p.m. dinner res at Le Veau D’or, with matching velvet trousers and elegant heeled mules.

In this enduring CBK look—one of most recognizable style references—a simple black V-neck meets a cream-colored skirt, patent brown boots, and no-fuss black leather handbag.

Chic yet comfy, this borrowed-from-the-boys look inspired by Princess Di off-duty ’80s style feels perfectly right for a lazy Sunday in the neighborhood.

A reason—or an excuse—to embrace winter whites. One of Jagger’s most iconic looks, this tonal ensemble is simple enough to put together. The key is to round out with accessories in complementary shades of cream and white.

How Parisians dress for a rainy day. We love this mini short and tights combo for milder winter weather.

A layered fall look built around soft neutrals that can easily go from day to night. Lift a page from Iman’s style book by adding a hat to the mix, in whatever shape best suits you.

This outfit from Audrey Hepburn on set for Sabrina might be one of the chicest of all time. Make it yours it a matching cinched-waist blazer and skirt, topped off with a hat, kitten heels, and door-knocker earrings. Terrier not included!

Between the cuffs, the leather gloves, and the oversized belt—here’s proof that all-black works best as a mix of textures.

Mexican artist Frida Kahlo loved a skirt; she was often photographed in a statement print or pattern. Recreate her look with a jacquard floral maxi, paired with a velvet top, lace gloves, and silver earrings.

How to Win Big Betting on Jensen Huang? Listen to Trump

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Just days ago, Jensen Huang’s chances of returning as Nvidia CEO looked increasingly slim, at least according to prediction markets. Earlier this month, Huang resigned from the AI hardware giant following reports of multiple extramarital affairs, including one with a woman alleged to have ties to Chinese intelligence services. As more reports emerged about Huang’s alleged bad behavior, Nvidia’s board insisted it had no plans to reinstate him. By early Sunday morning on the East Coast, traders on Polymarket’s US platform put his chances of returning at just 10 percent. But by Monday, Huang was back in charge.

The swings in Huang’s odds on prediction markets offer a window into how his unlikely comeback unfolded and who saw it coming. The New York Times reported that President Trump played a crucial role in convincing the board to bring Huang back. Traders who paid attention to what Trump and his allies wanted appear to have fared much better than those who did not.

On Sunday evening in Washington, Huang’s odds on Polymarket US jumped six percentage points in just 40 minutes after Trump published a Truth Social missive calling for Huang’s reinstatement. The president said he wanted to help revive the stock market, which had plunged following Huang’s scandal, a sign of Nvidia’s enormous influence over the AI industry and broader US economy.

According to the prediction market intelligence firm Betting Intelligence, odds on both Kalshi and Polymarket’s markets largely tracked with public signals about the likelihood of Huang’s return. They rose when Huang said he was open to returning, fell when the board dismissed the possibility, and climbed again when President Trump threw his support behind the former CEO. But there were several large trades that could not be tied to public developments.

Betting Intelligence and the prediction market forensics firm MSR Decode identified clusters of trades on Huang’s return that did not appear to be driven by news or public commentary. Kalshi and Polymarket declined to comment on whether they are currently investigating potential insider trading in markets related to Huang’s return.

Despite these anomalies, Betting Intelligence CEO Dan Zimmermann says the Huang-related markets showed fewer signs of potentially suspicious activity than usual. “The group that could have known the board’s decision in advance was small. So the market was mostly headline chasers and longshot buyers,” he says.

For traders who profited from Huang’s comeback, the lesson is clear: Don’t discount Trump.

Isaac Rose-Berman, a research and policy fellow at the American Institute for Boys & Men and professional sports bettor, says he made approximately $10,000 wagering on Huang’s return on Kalshi. He placed his trade after the Nvidia chief said on X that he was open to reclaiming his old job. Rose-Berman says that he figured that the board would consider Huang’s return if it looked likely to improve the company’s stock performance. (Never mind the concerns about national security or Huang allegedly threatening employees that he would “pee in your brain.”) “It’s 2026 and Trump is president, scandal schmandal,” Rose-Berman says. “Should’ve bet more.”

A pseudonymous prediction market trader known as Domer tells WIRED he cleared approximately $600,000 by placing an early wager last week on Huang’s return. He wasn’t focused explicitly on Trump’s desires, but he says his understanding of power dynamics ultimately guided his approach. “Boards can be very malleable,” he tells WIRED.

Other prediction market “sharps” say that commentary from Trumpworld was the strongest signal available about Huang’s return. As some bettors noted, there was a notable opportunity for profit after Trump suggested to a PBS NewsHour reporter that Huang would be back in power soon, but before Nvidia officially announced his reinstatement. “The key was looking at Trump’s support and the recent performance of the stock market,” says Kalshi trader Caleb Davies.

Aaron Courtney, a data analyst and cofounder of a prediction market analytics firm, says he took a loss on his Huang position. Looking back, he says his mistake was not paying attention to what Trump and other administration officials said over the weekend. “Trump saying he hoped Nvidia would bring Jensen back would be the clearest signal,” he says.

Disneyland’s Fantasmic Once Again Went Up In Flames, And I’m Starting To Worry About The Show’s Future

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Turns Out, When You Play A Disney Princess They Let You Sleep In Walt's Disneyland Apartment

Billie From Hexed Has Just Landed At Disney Parks, But The Directors Already Have Bigger Ideas

Unlock instant access to exclusive member features.

Get full access to premium articles, exclusive features and a growing list of member rewards.

Disneyland live entertainment is few and far between as of late, which is a shame considering the Anaheim theme park used to boast some of the best shows. On November 21st, one of the last and most beloved nighttime shows, Fantasmic, went up in flames for the second time in three short years. With two incidents and Disney’s desire to cut back on live entertainment, I’m really starting to worry about the future of the show.

The fire reportedly broke out during Fantasmic’s second performance of the night shortly before the show’s finale. While the Mark Twain finale was able to go on, flames can be seen behind the boat on the small stage set up on Tom Saywer Island. Guests at the park were quick to pull out their phones and film the growing flames as the Mark Twain, full of classic Disney characters, made its way around the river. Take a look:

Here’s the Mark Twain Riverboat running during Fantasmic last night with the fire behind it. This happened during the 10:30PM show https://t.co/bMcOZ6z0PtNovember 22, 2026

The last two minutes of the show were canceled, and guests were asked to vacate the viewing area while the fire department handled the blaze. Thankfully, the fire was small compared to the 2023 incident that turned the Maleficent dragon animatronic into a giant fireball and forever changed the show. There’s been no word on the extent of the damage this time around, but considering the show was back Sunday night it safe to assume that nothing was affected and no cast members were harmed, thankfully.

Two fiery incidents are definitely a cause for concern, and with Disneyland executives’ history of scaling back entertainment to cut costs, I can’t help but worry about the show’s fate. After all, Fantasmic boasts a large cast of incredible performers and several floating units. Plus water screens, projectors, and fireworks. I’m no expert, but I’m willing to bet it’s one of the most costly attractions at Disneyland. Not to mention, the show is still feeling the loss of the Maleficent Dragon, which makes the battle between Mickey and Maleficent lackluster.

While I don’t think Disneyland would end the show without also announcing a replacement, I do think Disney fans should prepare themselves. After all, Disney fans aren’t known for taking new changes well. Just look at the uproar that happened when it was announced Dinosaur would close to make room for a new Indiana Jones attraction at Disney World.

Disneyland has been having a rough go at things this month. On the first day of the holiday season, the Anaheim theme park’s iconic fake snow was accidentally turned on during a rainstorm, creating a mess. Back in October, Frontierland experienced widespread flooding. With park tickets being so expensive, it’s unfortunate to see cracks in Disneyland’s magic, but not even the mouse can control the weather or unexpected fires.

After pleading guilty to the 2022 cyberattacks that leaked Grand Theft Auto 6 to the public that year, 21-year-old hacker Arion Kurtaj has been let off with a warning from the Woolwich Crown Court.

According to the BBC, Kurtaj was initially set to receive seven years in prison, but has instead been freed with a warning from the judge who admonished him, “Don’t let this happen again.”Â

The shortened sentence comes after Kurtaj spent the past few years in a hospital, having originally been deemed unfit to stand trial due to his acute autism. In January, he was deemed fit for trial and moved to a standard prison, and after pleading guilty last week, he was sentenced and released on the same day. Kurtaj now has a Serious Crime Prevention Order which restricts his online activity and prevents him from using social media apps Telegram and Discord. The order will remain in effect for five years.

Kurtaj hacked into Rockstar, stealing and releasing the earliest known footage of Grand Theft Auto 6, in 2022. He was apparently able to pull off the hack with just an Amazon Fire Stick and a Bluetooth mouse and keyboard.Â

States sue SpaceX to stop Grok from generating child sex images, revenge porn

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

X says every Grok output is screened, but state AGs say Grok is still making CSAM.

On Monday, Virginia and Oregon became the first states to sue SpaceX, alleging that Grok continues to sexualize, unchecked, real photos of mostly women and children.

SpaceX acquired xAI in February and now must handle the ongoing reckoning over the controversial rollout of Grok’s “spicy mode”—a model feature Musk hyped but was soon found to generate a flood of non-consensual intimate imagery (NCII) and child sex abuse images (CSAM).

Initial safeguards were so lax that Grok sometimes generated nude images without being asked. Although X Safety and xAI (now known as SpaceXSI) were silent at first, Musk’s companies eventually apologized and claimed that additional safeguards were implemented and policies redesigned to block harmful outputs. However, state attorneys general alleged that those statements were “misleading” and that SpaceXSI instead quietly “embraced” sexualized content to seize more AI market share, as Grok continues to generate NCII and CSAM today.

Without intervention, kids in Virginia and Oregon risk a lifetime of distress, as Grok images will likely be impossible to remove online.

“Once created and shared,” Grok-generated CSAM and NCII “can never be fully erased, and it becomes a permanent record of the child’s abuse that may resurface for the rest of the child’s life,” Virginia Attorney General Jay Jones’ complaint said. “Virginia has a profound interest in protecting its children from this harm.”

Millions of residents were allegedly harmed, as the attorneys general seek to protect every Grok or X user in their state. Many Grok victims will likely suffer from chronic anxiety, depression, and PTSD, as well as reputational harm, as some viewers may not realize the images are AI-generated, the states alleged. Further—due to Grok’s integration with the social media platform X—users as young as 13 may have been exposed to the AI content and possibly re-traumatized if they, too, are victims of sexual abuse, the states alleged.

“xAI not only knowingly endangered kids and our communities, but they also lied to our faces about non-existent safety measures,” Oregon Attorney General Dan Rayfield said in a press release.

States alleged that xAI could have conducted rigorous red-teaming to block outputs before the new feature launched. But instead, the X Safety team responsible for Grok red-teaming was reduced to two or three team members, states alleged.

Overall, rather than use industry-standard safeguards, Musk’s firms allegedly put profits over women’s and children’s safety, states alleged, while complaining that SpaceXSI won’t even publicly identify “the officers responsible for safety governance.”

“Industry standard requires not only building guardrails but also vigilantly monitoring whether they hold,” states alleged. SpaceXSI is doing neither effectively, states alleged.

Asked for comment, SpaceXSI shared an X Safety post on X, which repeated claims that the company has a “zero-tolerance” policy for “any forms of child sexual exploitation.” X Safety said that the company “acted promptly to tighten” Grok protections, which are supposedly “designed to refuse any request to generate explicit images of minors or real people.” That statement also claimed that X Safety continually tests and adjusts protections when bad actors find ways to avoid filters, while relying on “sophisticated classifiers that screen every prompt” to catch harmful outputs “before an image is generated and check every output after.” Although the statement did not mention whether xAI sues users who break Grok rules to generate CSAM and NCII, it emphasized that xAI reports harmful content generated to police.

“When someone tries to break our safeguards and generate this content, we take appropriate measures, including actioning accounts and notifying law enforcement,” X Safety said. “Attempting to create this material is a serious crime, and the individuals who do it should be prosecuted to the fullest extent of the law. We will continue to work with the authorities via all available means to pursue these bad actors.”

However, state attorneys general alleged that reports from independent researchers and news outlets this year have shown that Grok is still generating NCII and CSAM. They noted that the chatbot “even advises users how to circumvent its own limited moderation measures to generate those images.” In some cases, a Virginia state probe found that Grok complied “with a user request to draft a prompt designed to avoid moderation.” So, even when filters work, the chatbot helps determined users immediately get around them, states alleged.

Nothing xAI did put a dent in the harmful outputs, least of all putting the feature behind a paywall, states alleged, which only ensured maximum profits. A troubled New York City Council speaker, Julie Menin, confronted SpaceX this October with proof that attempts to digitally undress her own photo still worked for free Grok users, states alleged.

Concluding its probe, Virginia found that moderation remains “inconsistent and ineffective,” with Grok sometimes carrying out prompts “that it later blocked.”

In its complaint, Virginia alleged that Grok protections “were weak, ineffective, sparsely enforced, and easily circumvented.” Oregon agreed that rather than act to protect users, SpaceX entities repeatedly made statements “with the intent to deceive consumers” that Grok was safe. Most egregiously to state attorneys general, in January, Elon Musk denied any harm to real people, claiming that Grok is only “supposed [to] allow upper body nudity of imaginary adult humans (not real ones) and only generated nudity of fake people (not real ones).”

“Contrary to Musk’s representation,” Grok “makes the same sexualizing edits to photographs of real, identifiable people,” states warned. “Defendants intentionally released a product that inundated their platforms with CSAM, NCII, and non-consensual sexualized images,” they alleged, and other “contemporaneous statements by Musk make clear that Grok’s ability to create sexualized material was part of xAI’s business strategy.”

It’s also troubling to states that the National Center for Missing and Exploited Children (NCMEC) reported to Congress that more than 90 percent of the Grok-generated CSAM that xAI submitted to its CyberTipline was considered a “dead end.”

As NCMEC explained, xAI initially refused to provide NCMEC and police with “basic user information, including location information, that law enforcement needs to act.” To fix the problem, NCMEC had to set up a meeting with xAI and get the reports resubmitted. This, states alleged, confirmed that “the information existed and that xAI had not provided it.” The concerning omission allegedly slowed down police response, just as the average number of Grok CSAM reports jumped by 88 percent from December 2025 to January 2026, then increased by another 66 percent from January to June 2026.

“X users were subjected to the risk that any person, at any time, could use their likeness to create CSAM, NCII, or other non-consensual sexualized images,” states alleged.

Lawmakers have asked state courts for an injunction blocking SpaceX entities from violating consumer protection laws by allegedly operating Grok without industry standard safeguards. If they win, they want SpaceX to pay thousands in civil penalties per violation, as well as disgorge any ill-gotten gains from marketing the chatbot as safe to users.

“Most people who use social media do not want to contribute to the harm caused to victims of CSAM, NCII, and other non-consensual sexualized images by being exposed to those images online,” states alleged.

Although Oregon’s complaint only vaguely estimates the number of users Grok attracted with Musk’s allegedly deceitful marketing strategy as at least “hundreds of thousands,” Virginia’s complaint included more detailed estimates. It said that approximately 6.4 million X accounts were linked to geolocations in the state, while the Grok application was downloaded 986,624 times by users with Virginia-based IP addresses.

“When xAI allowed Grok’s creators to sideline consent to turn a profit, they prioritized growth and engagement over safety, and they deceived the public about it,” Jones said. “Today’s filings are the first step towards holding this company accountable.”

After all, you don’t need to know everything, only what’s important.

7 Thanksgiving Scraps to Save for Backyard Birds – Give Them a Helping Hand This Holiday

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Join The Club for quick access. Enter your email below and we'll send confirmation, and sign you up to our newsletter.

Keep exploring and earning more as a member.

Beautiful plants and ideas to brighten your garden.

Expert tips for growing fruit, vegetables and herbs at home.

Sign up to get fresh gardening ideas, inspiration and expert advice delivered straight to your inbox.

Want To Befriend a Squirrel? Here's How To Attract These Cute Critters To Your Yard and Earn Their Trust

Why Birds Peck Windows in Fall – the Real Reasons Revealed by Experts

What Your Coneflowers Need in November – 5 Tips to Get Them Winter-Ready and Help Backyard Birds

Sign up for the Gardening Know How newsletter today and receive a free copy of our e-book "How to Grow Delicious Tomatoes".

Unlock instant access to exclusive member features.

Get full access to premium articles, exclusive features and a growing list of member rewards.

Your Thanksgiving feast could provide a few tasty treats for garden birds. Just make sure you know which leftovers are safe to serve and which should stay off the menu.

When you purchase through links on our site, we may earn an affiliate commission. Here’s how it works.

The holidays often leave us with more food than we know what to do with, don't they? Before throwing everything into the compost or bin, though, take a look at your Thanksgiving leftovers; your feathered friends could be very thankful for them!

Yes, some everyday ingredients from the feasting table can make welcome treats for backyard birds, offering a little extra nourishment as temperatures drop and the nights draw in (and transform your outdoor space into a welcoming bird sanctuary in the process).

Whether you're hoping to see your birth month bird out there, or just happy to offer your kitchen scraps up to any local critters who might make use of them, here's how to use your Thanksgiving leftovers to keep bird feeders filled over the holidays...

Before we dive on in, a word of caution: not every Thanksgiving dish is suitable for wildlife, especially as salt, butter, rich sauces and seasonings can make seemingly harmless scraps unsuitable for birds.

The trick? Try to save a few simple ingredients before they make their way into the casserole dish or onto the dinner table. With that caveat in place, then, here are seven Thanksgiving kitchen scraps worth keeping...

Have a few apples left over from your Thanksgiving preparations? Birds such as robins and other fruit-eating visitors will definitely appreciate small pieces of fresh apple.

Sign up for the Gardening Know How newsletter today and receive a free copy of our e-book "How to Grow Delicious Tomatoes".

Cut the flesh into bite-sized chunks, removing the core and seeds first. Bruised apples are fine so long as they're fresh (they're not as fussy as we are!), but discard any mouldy or spoiled pieces. And avoid offering apple pie filling or cooked apples covered in sugar and spices.

Cranberries are a Thanksgiving staple, and you'd better believe that their fresh (and unseasoned, obviously) counterparts make a lovely treat for birds. Offer a few whole or chopped cranberries on a clean feeding tray like this one from MIXXIDEA.

Frozen cranberries are fine, too, once thawed. Just avoid cranberry sauce, as it typically contains added sugar and may include other ingredients birds don't need. Honestly, a little fresh fruit is all that's required.

Pecans (again, plain and unsalted) are another holiday favorite that can be shared with feathered visitors, along with walnuts and peanuts. All of these scrummy nuts are rife at Thanksgiving, and all provide fats and protein that can help birds maintain their energy during colder weather.

Take care to chop larger nuts into manageable pieces and check that they haven't been coated in salt, sugar, chocolate or seasoning. Just like the fruit, nuts should be fresh, too, rather than stale or musty.

Corn often features on the Thanksgiving menu in a big way, and a few plain kernels can be offered to garden birds; just set aside a small portion before adding butter, salt or other seasonings.

Cooked or fresh corn kernels can be placed on a feeding tray, but avoid creamed corn, buttery corn casserole and heavily seasoned dishes. Only offer a little at a time, as damp corn can spoil quickly.

If pumpkin or squash features in your holiday cooking (or your Thanksgiving decor, if you're Kristin Davis!), don't throw away all those seeds; instead, rinse away any stringy pulp, remove any seasoning and allow the seeds to dry thoroughly.

Offer them (have I gotten too repetitive yet?) plain and unsalted, either whole for larger birds or broken into smaller pieces for smaller visitors. They're a useful source of fats and nutrients, making them a worthwhile addition to your bird feeding station.

Leftover rice can also be shared with birds from your Thanksgiving feast, provided it hasn't been cooked with salt, butter, oil, garlic or onions. Both white and brown rice are suitable in small quantities when prepared plainly.

P.S. There's a persistent myth that uncooked or cooked rice swells inside birds and causes harm, but this isn't true. Nevertheless, rice shouldn't replace more nutritious bird food, so just offer just a small amount (you may find your birth month winter bird adores it) and remove anything uneaten so it doesn't have a chance to go bad.

A little leftover boiled or baked potato from your Thanksgiving prep can be another treat for your feathered friends. Set aside a portion before adding butter, milk, salt or gravy, then allow it to cool and chop it into small pieces.

Please never offer raw potatoes or green potato peelings, and avoid chips, roast potatoes cooked in fat and heavily seasoned mashed potatoes. Experts always advise that plain potato be treated as an occasional extra rather than a regular food source.

It's every bit as important to know what to avoid when it comes to feeding birds your Thanksgiving leftovers. Turkey fat and pan drippings might seem like an easy substitute for bird suet, but they're best kept off the feeding station. Audubon, in fact, warns that turkey fat melts more readily than traditional suet and can turn rancid quickly. It can also smear onto feathers, potentially affecting their waterproofing and insulation.

Gravy, stuffing and salty side dishes should also stay off the menu, as they often contain butter, salt, onion and garlic, none of which are suitable additions to a wild bird's diet. Avoid, too, anything that contains chocolate, along with avocado, sugary desserts and heavily processed foods, too. Bread and pastry aren't particularly nutritious (even if they won't do any harm), so think up some better ways to use your leftovers; might this author humbly submit the idea of a bread-and-butter pudding?

Once you've picked all of the bird-friendly scraps out of your Thanksgiving feast, be sure to present it properly! Always use a clean feeding tray rather than leaving piles of food on the ground, and try to put food out in the morning, as this is when it's most likely to be eaten.

Take care to remove anything uneaten before it spoils, and wash feeding surfaces regularly to reduce the risk of bacteria and disease spreading. It's also worth keeping fresh drinking water available, particularly as colder weather approaches, and remember that kitchen scraps are supposed to be occasional extras, not a replacement for a balanced wild bird food mix.

Still, all those warnings aside, there's no denying that sharing a little of your holiday feast with your local birds is a lovely way to give thanks to Mother Nature (and reduce food waste at the same time).

She previously served as digital editor at Stylist magazine, and has written extensively for Ideal Home, Woman & Home, Homes & Gardens, and a handful of other titles. Kayleigh is passionate about wildlife-friendly gardening, and recently cancelled her weekend plans to build a mini pond when her toddler found a frog living in their water barrel. As such, her garden – designed around the stunning magnolia tree at its centre – is filled to the brim with pollinator-friendly blooms, homemade bird feeders, and old logs for insects to nest in.

10 Fun 2026 Games That Would Make for Great Holiday Gifts

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Eric Warner is a Journalist and Multimedia Producer based in New England with over 7 years of experience producing stories for multiple publications

For many cultures and countries across the globe, the holiday season is in full swing, with many households preparing to attend family gatherings, celebrate events, and, of course, starting to purchase holiday gifts for children and other loved ones.

Several storefronts and digital retailers have already begun issuing early Black Friday and Cyber Monday deals, with many products, especially physical video games, going on sale for 25-50% off their original sale price.

With 2026 being a particularly great year for new video game releases, here are 10 fun new video games that were released this year that would make for great holiday gifts for loved ones of all kinds ranging from titles intended for children to those aimed at more mature gamers.

Mojang’s Minecraft is considered by gamers of all ages to be one of the greatest games of all time, and over the years it’s been expanded upon through numerous updates. If fans want to get an early look at the game’s first new dimension in 15 years, then they’ll have to check out Minecraft Dungeons II.

Like its 2020 predecessor, Minecraft Dungeons II is a third-person hack and slash dungeon crawler. It follows a hero as they set out to stop illagers from threatening the world with power from the new Warden-based Sift dimension, fighting a variety of new exclusive mobs along the way.

While Minecraft Dungeons II has only been out for less than two months so far and isn’t currently on sale, it would surely make a great holiday gift for any fan of Minecraft, especially kids, as it does feature local four-player co-op allowing siblings, parents, and friends to easily play together.

Avengers: Doomsday is expected to be the biggest movie release of the decade. While Marvel fans still have to wait till late December to see the film, they can quench their thirst for action-packed Marvel content by purchasing Marvel Tokon: Fighting Souls.

Set in the Japanese anime-inspired Earth-358, Marvel Tokon: Fighting Souls is a 2D 4v4 tag team fighting game centered around the Fighting Avengers, the Unbreakable X-Men, the Amazing Guardians, the Samurai Outriders, and the Knights of Doom fighting each other to ultimately defeat the Champion of the Universe.

Marvel Tokon is easily the best Marvel video game released this year. Not only does it feature over 20 playable heroes and villains, but it also includes five distinct campaigns following each team attempting to defeat the Champion, with the best arguably following Doctor Doom and his cadre of iconic villains.

2026 has been great for horror titles, as fantastic games like The Sinking City 2 and Resident Evil Requiem debuted earlier this year. However, I would argue that the best horror game to gift horror fans this holiday season is Tarsier Studios’ Reanimal.

Similar in gameplay and visual design to the Little Nightmares series, Reanimal is a co-op platforming survival horror game following a young brother and sister as they travel to their home island to find three friends. Tragically, they find it in ruins and filled with gigantic humanoid and animalistic monsters.

Some horror stories are best experienced alone, but Reanimal is one that family and friends alike will certainly enjoy playing through together as they tackle puzzles and try to solve the mystery surrounding the island and its inhabitants.

This fall will be a lot of fun for couch and online co-op enthusiasts.

Millions of fans around the world love Star Wars. While Disney is releasing a ton of different SW products this holiday season to tantalize shoppers, people should consider gifting their Star Wars and video game fan loved ones with the surprise hit, Star Wars: Galactic Racer.

A roguelite racing game set shortly after the Battle of Jakku, Star Wars: Galactic Racer is centered around the rise of unsanctioned races via the new Galactic Racing League, with the protagonist Shade attempting to become the new Galactic League champion.

Galactic Racer’s colorful cast of characters and intense races utilizing podracers, land speeders, speeder bikes, or skim speeders have quickly made it become one of the most critically acclaimed SW titles in years. Fans will surely fall in love with the game for the hilarious character of Biddy Blas alone.

LEGO products have become a signature gift for decades now. While many parents will undoubtedly buy their kids a few LEGO sets this holiday season, they should also consider buying them the LEGO video game, LEGO Batman: Legacy of the Dark Knight.

Unlike past TT LEGO titles, LEGO Batman: Legacy of the Dark Knight isn’t based on any particular Batman movie but is rather a conglomeration of all things Batman, utilizing a gameplay structure similar to Batman: Arkham Knight while using elements from several Batman films, comics, and shows.

Legacy may not feature as many playable characters as past TT games, but it more than makes up for it by featuring dozens of alternate costumes for each character, alternate driveable variations of the Batmobile, and a customizable Batcave on top of returning two-player co-op.

Some gamers enjoy first-person shooters while others enjoy simulators, but some players want more open-world, narrative-driven experiences such as those commonly found in role-playing games. Arguably the best RPG of 2026 is Rebel Wolves’ debut title, The Blood of Dawnwalker.

Made by the creators of The Witcher 3: Wild Hunt, The Blood of Dawnwalker is a medieval fantasy RPG set in the 14th century kingdom of Vale Sangora, following a half-man half-vampire named Coen as he sets out to save his family and his country from the clutches of the Ancient Roman vampire Brencis.

The story, characters, and world-building of Dawnwalker are absolutely incredible, but it also has a ton of replay value as players can invest in witchcraft, sword skills, or vampire abilities and even accidentally drain the blood of NPCs if they don’t maintain their vampiric thirst.

As we approach the end of 2026, our lives are going to get even busier as the days pass, so why not add a hundred hour adventure or two to the mix?

Many older gamers are more action-oriented, and while 2026 has seen many great action games come out over the past few months, like Assassin's Creed Black Flag Resynced and Call of Duty: Modern Warfare 4, I would argue that best action game to gift gamers this holiday season is Gears of War: E-Day.

The sixth mainline entry in the third-person Gears of War series and prequel to the first Gears game, Gears of War: E-Day follows COG soldiers Marcus Fenix, Dominic Santiago, Mags Carter, and Lucas Reyes as they struggle to fight a horrifying invasion from the subterranean Locust Horde on Emergence Day.

E-Day may only be available on PC and Xbox Series X|S consoles, but it's by far the best Xbox exclusive released in years thanks to its gruesome, action-packed, yet emotional co-op campaign, thrilling PvP multiplayer, and addictive PvE Horde Siege mode.

With Legendary Pictures and Toho releasing several great Godzilla movies in recent years, many kids are growing up as huge Godzilla fans. A perfect gift to get them this holiday season is Godzilla: Destroy All Monsters Melee Remastered.

A remaster of 2002’s Godzilla: Destroy All Monsters Melee, Godzilla: Destroy All Monsters Melee Remastered is a four-player 3D fighting game with players being able to control some of Toho's most famous kaiju in city-destroying battles. Such behemoths as Godzilla, Rodan, King Ghidorah, Mechagodzilla, and more are playable.

What makes Godzilla: DAMMR an especially great gift this holiday season is its affordable price and small file size, with the game currently being sold on PS5 for $29.99 and featuring a file size of about 5 GB, making it a quick game to download and start playing.

Similar to LEGO, Pokemon has increasingly become a prevalent presence in households during the holiday season. While there are several Pokemon products to gift people this season, I would recommend people gift Pokemon Pokopia to fans.

Unlike the main turn-based RPG games, Pokemon Pokopia is social simulation game akin to Animal Crossing, with players embodying a human-imitating Ditto waking up in a post-apocalyptic version of the Kanto region and setting out to restore the area using its transformative powers.

What follows is an adorably charming experience as Ditto rebuilds habitats and befriends Pokemon from every generation, with people even able to create their own structures and minigames similar to Minecraft to play with friends.

From unforgettable adventures to clever dungeons, these games capture some of the magic that makes Zelda so special.

One of the best games to gift anyone this holiday season is undoubtedly the 2026 masterpiece that is The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time remake for the Nintendo Switch 2.

Released to celebrate The Legend of Zelda’s 40th anniversary, 2026’s The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time is a faithful modern remake of the classic Nintendo 64 action-adventure game, with the game once again following a young Link as he set out to save the kingdom of Hyrule from the evil Ganondorf.

Being a first-party Nintendo game that’s only a few weeks old, it’s unlikely that the Ocarina of Time remake will go on sale anytime soon, but it’s well worth purchasing at full price. Especially for parents who want to introduce their kids to Link’s most famous adventure for the first time.

The Game Of The Year nominees at The Game Awards 2026 have been revealed, and it's full of titles that launched near the end of the year.

Newcastle United and Chelsea are reportedly preparing to battle for the signature of highly-rated Hearts youngster Jaedon Donkor ahead of the January transfer window.

The 17-year-old winger has attracted considerable attention following his impressive performances in Scotland, with several clubs monitoring his development ahead of a potential move.

According to a Football Insider report, both Premier League clubs are exploring the possibility of bringing Donkor to England in January.

The teenager is approaching the final months of his contract at Tynecastle, with Hearts keen to secure his long-term future and fend off interest from potential suitors.

Donkor’s contract situation has put several clubs on alert, with Newcastle and Chelsea among those keen to exploit the uncertainty surrounding his future.

The London-born attacker previously spent time in the youth academies of Arsenal and Manchester United before joining Hearts, and a return to English football could now be on the cards.

However, the Premier League duo are not alone in their pursuit, with several European clubs also believed to be keeping tabs on the youngster.

Although he has not yet made his senior debut, the winger has shown considerable potential in youth football.

He has made an encouraging start to the campaign in the Scottish Challenge Cup, registering five goals in six appearances, including an impressive hat-trick against Kelty Hearts.

His attacking contributions have attracted attention from scouts across Europe, with his performances suggesting he could have a bright future in the game.

Newcastle and Chelsea are both understood to believe that Donkor has the potential to develop into a Premier League player.

Hearts will be determined to avoid losing one of their most promising prospects, but their contract situation could complicate negotiations.

A January move could therefore give either English club an opportunity to secure an exciting young talent before his value increases.

CHICAGO, Nov. 23, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- Cboe Global Markets, Inc. (Cboe: CBOE), a leading global markets operator and pioneer in equity and index derivatives, today announced that Craig Donohue, Chief Executive Officer, Prashant Bhatia, Executive Vice President and Head of Enterprise Strategy & Corporate Development, Jill Griebenow, Executive Vice President and Chief Financial Officer, and Rob Hocking, Executive Vice President and Global Head of Derivatives, will present at the Goldman Sachs Financial Services Conference in New York City on Wednesday, December 9, at 10:00 a.m. ET.

Lewis-Potter scores twice as Brentford thump four past 10-man Everton

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

Brentford vs Everton. Premier League.

Report and free match highlights from the Premier League clash between Brentford and Everton at the Gtech Community Stadium on Monday night; Keane Lewis-Potter scores twice and James Tarkowski sent off as Bees beat Toffees.

Keane Lewis-Potter’s first-half double set Brentford on their way to a comprehensive 4-0 Premier League win over 10-man Everton.

For David Moyes it was a record-setting night to forget. His 593rd game in charge of Everton, more than any other Toffees manager, ended in misery, as James Tarkowski's red card at 2-0 down left them with no hope of a comeback at the Gtech Community Stadium on Monday night.

Vitaly Janelt added a third for Brentford, while Fabio Carvalho came off the bench to score a fourth, as they moved up to sixth in the table, a point above Everton who slipped to ninth.

It was a deserved breakthrough for Brentford midway through the first half. A lovely touch from Jaidon Anthony teed up Lewis-Potter, who saw his strike deflect past Jordan Pickford.

Four minutes later it was 2-0. This time it came from a free-kick as the ball fell to Lewis-Potter. His first effort was blocked, but he got a second bite at it, and again a deflection from his strike took the ball away from the Everton goalkeeper.

It got worse for the Toffees before the break, too. Tarkowski was first given a yellow card for his follow-through challenge on Yehor Yarmolyuk, before a VAR check saw the referee upgrade it to red.

Moyes introduced Jack Grealish at the start of the second half, his first appearance since being dropped from the squad last month, but it would make minimal difference.

Janelt's rocket on 67 minutes added a Brentford third, as he picked out the top corner from 25 yards, while substitute Carvalho prodded in from a corner in stoppage time to add the icing to the cake.

Senegal-Morocco: CAS verdict, Romain Molina makes a major announcement

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

The soap opera surrounding the controversial CAN 2025 final between Senegal and Morocco will soon reach its epilogue. This Monday, the Court of Arbitration for Sport (CAS) announced through a press release that its verdict will be revealed this Tuesday afternoon, ten months after the meeting.

Since this CAS announcement, speculation and false information have been rife on social networks. This is why Romain Molina wanted to warn against the many rumors currently circulating.

“Anything that can currently circulate on the networks must not be taken into account”wrote the independent journalist on his X account. A message intended to calm speculation as the wait around this affair continues to grow.

As a reminder, the Senegalese Football Federation (FSF) contacted the CAS after the decision of the Confederation of African Football (CAF) to award a victory on the green carpet to Morocco (3-0). The Senegalese contest this decision, taken after the incidents that occurred during the final, initially won on the field (1-0 after) by the Lions of Teranga.

CrowdStrike to buy Zscaler in record-setting $43.9B cybersecurity deal

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

CrowdStrike Holdings Inc. today announced plans to acquire Zscaler Inc., a fellow provider of cybersecurity software, for about $43.9 billion.

The all-stock deal values the latter company at $260.4 per share. That’s a 34% premium to Zscaler’s closing price on Friday, the day CrowdStrike’s takeover interest became public. The transaction is the largest cybersecurity acquisition on record by a wide margin.

The companies reported their quarterly results in conjunction with the acquisition announcement. Both topped expectations across the board. CrowdStrike posted adjusted net income of $354.9 million on $1.58 billion in revenue, while Zscaler reported a $208.4 million adjusted profit and sales of $949.3 million. The former company also boosted its full-year guidance.

CrowdStrike’s flagship Falcon platform uses lightweight programs called sensors to protect employee devices, cloud instances and other technology assets. Zscaler’s Zero Trust Exchange, in turn, protects the network connections that link together those assets. The platform is positioned as an alternative to virtual private network tools.

The Zero Trust Exchange encrypts the traffic that flows between employee devices and business applications. When needed, administrators can decrypt the data to search for threats. Additionally, they can configure the platform to require more thorough authentication when users log into particularly sensitive systems.

The product portfolios of CrowdStrike’s and Zscaler overlap in some areas. Both companies offer tools that can protect artificial intelligence applications from threats such as prompt injections. They also compete in the secure browser category, although their respective offerings take different approaches to blocking malware.

Zscaler offers a tool that can automatically launch simulated cyberattacks against applications to find weak points. CrowdStrike joined the fray as well earlier this year with SafeMind, a set of custom AI models and related assets. It not only finds software vulnerabilities but also develops detections, automated workflows designed to spot hacking attempts.

CrowdStrike said that it will use the network telemetry collected by Zscaler to enhance SafeMind’s training dataset. In 2024, the latter company disclosed that its platform blocked more than 9 billion threats per day. That activity, which has no doubt grown in volume since, should give CrowdStrike’s AI models a more complete understanding of hacker behavior.

The company’s plans for Zscaler also extend beyond AI training. CrowdStrike will incorporate the network protection provider’s software into its Flex offering, which enables customers to buy multiple cybersecurity tools via a single contract. That could help expand the adoption of Zscaler’s products among CrowdStrike customers.

The companies have also identified other upselling opportunities. They disclosed today that about 8,000 Zscaler customers don’t yet use CrowdStrike products.

“With Zscaler, we’re building cybersecurity’s operating system: a single control plane to deploy AI, control AI, and enable any agentic application to run safely,” said CrowdStrike founder and Chief Executive Officer George Kurtz (pictured). “We see a path to become the largest AI safety and security company the world has ever seen.”

CrowdStrike expects to complete the acquisition in the second half of its 2028 fiscal year, which starts next February. The company stated that the deal will be “significantly and immediately accretive” to free cash flow per share upon closing.

CrowdStrike expects Zscaler’s financial impact to grow over time. It estimates that the deal will unlock $500 million worth of annual cost synergies within 18 to 24 months, mainly in the form of data center, supplier and operational improvements. The company also sees Zscaler boosting its annual revenue run-rate by $1 billion within 30 months.

Goodbye, Mandy: GTA 6 Players Are Already Obsessed With Someone Else

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: World  |  Read Source →

As Grand Theft Auto 6 began releasing around the world last week, players quickly became obsessed with Mandy. She's a neighbor of Jason's who tries to seduce the himbo while he fixes her sink. (And she is totally not a serial killer, no way, why would you ask?) Mandy shows up early in GTA 6. But as more players advance in the game, they're becoming obsessed with a new character: Vicki, a standout character from Chapter 2.

[Ed. note: Light spoilers for the end of Chapter 1 and beginning of Chapter 2 follow. We'll keep them vague.]

After Jason is injured at the end of Chapter 1, Lucia takes him to a run-down motel to lie low. She wants to find stronger painkillers for him, which leads her to Vicki. She's a goth exotic dancer who also moonlights as a small-time drug dealer, and immediately pulls one over on Lucia by selling her over-the-counter Zombix (GTA's brand of painkiller) for $50. Lucia then barges back into Vicki's room and demands the drugs she paid for, which takes the two on a trip around Port Gellhorn. They get Vicki's paycheck from Delights Cabaret and save her friend Dunk from a gang, all the while getting to know one another. They bond over the fact that they're both moms trying to support their kids by whatever means necessary.

Players have quickly fallen for Vicki. "Love her she's my favorite character so far," one player wrote on Reddit.

"Punk Ana de Armas," another said, and, you know, I can kinda see it.

Vicki is voiced by Taryn Manning, who's best known for her role in Orange Is the New Black. She's also starred in 8 Mile, Hustle and Flow, and Hawaii Five-0.

Manning's work as Vicki is part of the reason why players are flocking to her. "Mandy didn't do it for me, but Vicki did because I recognized Taryn Manning’s voice immediately," one Redditor said.

"Vicki the baddie that made everyone move on from Mandy lmao," another said.

Basically, you can tell how far thirsty GTA 6 players are getting in the game based on who they're obsessively posting about over on the Grand Theft Auto subreddits. In a few days, as players continue to marathon Rockstar's hit, I'm sure they'll have a new favorite.

If you've been patiently waiting for the right moment to pull the trigger on the Nintendo Switch 2 after the recent release of The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time, this might be a good time to do so. Amazon is selling a Nintendo Switch 2 Mario Kart World Bundle for $499 (originally $549.99) and Woot is selling a regular Nintendo Switch 2 for $469 (originally $499). Both discounts come as the first major discount since the Sept. 1 price hike.

The new console is an upgrade in every way, as you can read in our full review of the Nintendo Switch 2. The ergonomics and design have improved, making it much better to hold and look at. The battery life has also improved, now with about 180 minutes of handheld playtime before the juice runs out.

A big plus is that the Switch 2 is backwards-compatible: You can play your old Switch games on it. Some Switch games have the ability to upgrade to the Switch 2 Edition by buying that game's upgrade pack. This is especially worth it for games like The Legend of Zelda: Tears of the Kingdom, which will look better on the Switch 2.

There are a lot more games out for the Switch 2 now than there were at the beginning of the year. Nintendo has hinted that they're prioritizing making more classic remakes, with many more on the pipeline, so there are plenty of games to keep you busy for a while, like Donkey Kong Bananza, Kirby Air Riders, and Hyrule Warriors: Age of Imprisonment, which are already out. How Becoming A Mom Is Already Affecting Hailee Steinfeld Picking New Movie Roles

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Join The Club for quick access. Enter your email below and we'll send confirmation, and sign you up to our newsletter.

Here's what the Hawkeye actress is doing "moving forward".

When you purchase through links on our site, we may earn an affiliate commission. Here’s how it works.

Hailee Steinfeld just became a mama earlier this year when her and her husband Josh Allen welcomed their daughter in the spring. It was just confirmed that she’s got not one but two projects among upcoming 2026 movies – now that the final Avengers: Doomsday trailer showed Kate Bishop among the massive roster of heroes. But will her new starring role in her daughter’s life change how she approaches Hollywood going forward?

Most new mothers have to ask themselves the question of how work might change their lives once they become one. Here’s what Steinfeld said about it when it was posed to her by Entertainment Tonight:

I guess I’ve always made projects with the thought in my mind somewhere in there, maybe not necessarily at the front of it, but that like, I’ll get to share this with my family someday.

The beginning of Steinfeld’s answer reminds me how she famously decided to explore her own mixed heritage roots when she got to play Mary in Sinners. The actress said that the research for that role was looking into her family history, and that brought her closer to her mother and her grandfather, who account for the Filipino and African American side of her ancestry. These days she’s going to also think of her daughter too when she picks jobs, too. As Steinfeld said herself:

And now, I do feel like maybe moving forward that’ll be something I strongly take into account. But, the fact that [Hexed] was the first thing that came into my life and the first project that she is really a part of is really special.

Steinfeld’s latest movie is Walt Disney Animation’s latest, Hexed, which is all about a mother and her teen daughter at its core against a backdrop of a world of witches. The 29-year-old has shared before that the role came at a time where a story about “an overprotective mother makes a hell of a lot more sense to me” than it would have before this phase of her life.

In the movie, Steinfeld plays Billie, a misfit teenager who finds out she’s been a witch her whole life, but that her powers have been concealed by her mom with a bracelet. Rashida Jones voices the role of her mother along with Jodie Foster playing an evil witch named Celeste and Walton Goggins playing a three-eyed cat named Beef Roger. Hexed has earned positive reactions ahead of its release this Wednesday, November 25.

Outside of her latest role, Steinfeld is confirmed to reprise her role as Kate Bishop in Avengers: Doomsday, hitting theaters on December 18. Then we’ll hear her voice again as Gwen Stacy/Spider-Gwen in Spider-Man: Beyond the Spider-Verse coming out on June 18. We’re so curious what’s next for Steinfeld in her new mom era! For now, she’s staying booked and busy even as she enjoys some much-deserved time off with her baby daughter, Harper Haize Allen.

NYC Mayor’s Office Hires First Executive Director of Music

📅 Nov 23, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Rachael Pazdan will help the city support musicians by serving as a liaison between the government and the music industry.

The NYC mayor’s office of media and entertainment (MOME) hired Rachael Pazdan as its first executive director of music. She will report to Rafael Espinal, commissioner of the NYC MOME.

“New York City has shaped the sound of music for generations,” Espinal said in a statement. “But being a great city for music should also mean being a great city for the people who make it. That’s why building our music portfolio is a priority for me. Rachael Pazdan brings the experience and relationships to help us get it right. Together, we’ll listen to the industry and work toward making live performances easier, expanding opportunities across all five boroughs, and connecting young New Yorkers to careers in music. Our goal is simple: make New York City the best place in the world to create music, perform music, and make a living in music.”

Pazdan will build on the city’s New York Music Month and its newly launched Music Production Bootcamp, as well as Mayor Zohran Mamdani’s commitment to increase access to entertainment experiences for NYC residents.

Since launching in 2017, New York Music Month has grown to host 38 talks, 21 performances, a conference and a free rehearsal series that offers more than 2,000 hours of free rehearsal space across the city in four locations.

Parzdan has worked with indie music promoter AdHoc, helping the company become a full-service creative agency that served clients including Bryant Park, Pitchfork, A2IM and BandLab. While at the company, she also helped promote events including Global Citizen in New York City and Johannesburg.

She also served as a booker for numerous independent venues across New York City, including Public Records, The Bell House and Le Poisson Rouge, and produced the short-form docuseries Stories from the Stage, which shared stories from New York City’s independent venues and promoters during the pandemic.
